# THE EXAM DOCKET — Deuteronomy 11:1-32 (the discipline retold — the exodus, the sea, Dathan and Abiram, the wilderness; the land watered by heaven — hills and valleys, the rain of heaven, the eyes of the LORD from the year's beginning to its end; the second paragraph — hearken, the rain in its season, the early and the late, grass for the cattle, eat and be satisfied, take heed lest, the heavens shut, these words on the heart, the sign, the frontlets, the teaching, the doorposts, the days multiplied as the days of the heavens; the borders and the dread — keep and love and walk and cleave, the nations dispossessed, every place the sole treads, the wilderness to Lebanon, the river to the western sea, no man shall stand, the dread and the fear, as He spoke; the blessing and the curse — see, I set before you today, Gerizim and Ebal beyond the Jordan opposite Gilgal by the terebinths of Moreh, cross and possess and dwell, keep to do) — THE DEUTERONOMY WALK sitting 9b, THE DOCKET'S OWN RUN (2026-09-20; DEUTERONOMY_WALK.md "Sitting 9b — THE COMPILE OF CHAPTER 11 … THE DESIGN", THE ORDER)

- Rosh Hashanah 8b:6 [LINK — Deut 11:12] — LAW. the sabbatical year from Tishri — 'year' (Leviticus 25:4) / 'year' (11:12 'from the beginning of the year') the verbal analogy (I2): the year that begins with the rainy season: F2 — 11:12's 'year' the calendar's anchor by analogy; calendar by CALL.
- Rosh Hashanah 9b:11 [LINK — Deut 11:12 — CREDITED: holiness_b_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md] — LAW. CREDITED (holiness_b_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md — its form carries no verdict; read whole here) — the New Year for planting: orla (the tree's first three years — Leviticus 19:23-24) counted from Tishri by 'year' / 'year' with 11:12 'from the beginning of the year' (I2): F2 — 11:12 the calendar's anchor a second time; holiness_b by CALL.
- Rosh Hashanah 16b:3 [LINK — Deut 11:12] — DERIVATION. R. Yitzchak: a year poor at its beginning is made rich at its end — 'from the beginning' (11:12) written defectively, read 'poverty' ('rashut'); 'until the end' the good end: F2 — a do-not-read reading on the spelling (named, no code); the year's arc — the exam's row.
- Rosh Hashanah 17b:11 [LINK — Deut 11:12] — LAW. THE CROWN OF F2: the baraita — 'a land the LORD your God cares for; His eyes always on it' (11:12): providence for good and for bad — wholly wicked at Rosh Hashanah, meager rain decreed, then they repented: the decree cannot be added to, but He brings the sparse rain AT THE RIGHT TIMES on the land that needs it, as good as much: F2 — THE DECREE FIXED AT THE HEAD OF THE YEAR, ITS DISTRIBUTION FREE — rain_in_its_season's 'in its season' the free variable after the decree; 17b:12 the mirror.

## Taanit
- Taanit 2a:11 [LINK — Deut 11:13 Deut 11:14] — LAW. the mention of rain in the Amida (the standing prayer) — 'to love the LORD your God and to serve Him with all your heart' (11:13): the service in the heart is PRAYER; then 'and I will give the rain of your land in its season' (11:14): rain asked in the service of the heart: F3 — THE PRAYER FROM THE PARAGRAPH'S OWN CLAUSE AND THE RAIN ATTACHED TO IT — the liturgy's derivation from 11:13-14; rain_dates' first row (Mishnah Ta'anit 1:1 through 2a:1).
- Taanit 3b:6 [LINK — Deut 11:17] — DISPUTE. Rav Yosef's baraita: 'He will shut up the heavens' (11:17) — from the clouds and the winds, since 'no rain' is already said: F3 — heavens_shut_for_turning's VALUE: the shutting is clouds and winds withheld, not rain alone.
- Taanit 6a:3 [LINK — Deut 11:14] — DERIVATION. 'yoreh' (the early rain) for a blessing, not for washing away — proved from 'malkosh' (the late rain), and the late from the early (11:14): the pair reads each other: F3 — the two named rains both for blessing — rain_in_its_season's value.
- Taanit 6a:5 [LINK — Deut 11:14] — LAW. the early rain in Marcheshvan and the late rain in Nisan — 'in its due time' (11:14), not Tishri and Iyar: F3 — rain_dates' arms — THE PARAMETER'S ROW.
- Taanit 6a:7 [LINK — Deut 11:14] — LAW. the early rain in Marcheshvan, not Kislev — 'the early rain and the late rain' (11:14): as the late falls in its time so the early; after Nisan a curse: F3 — rain_dates (Marcheshvan the early).
- Taanit 6b:8 [LINK — Deut 11:17] — LAW. R. Abbahu: the first rainfall saturates a handbreadth, the second seals a barrel's mouth with its mud; Rav Chisda: rains that seal a barrel's mouth are not 'He will shut the heavens and there be no rain' (11:17): F3 — heavens_shut_for_turning's THRESHOLD (a measure of mud) — a PARAMETER row.
- Taanit 7b:5 [LINK — Deut 11:17] — DERIVATION. R. Tanchum: rain withheld only when Israel is sentenced to destruction (Job 24:19); Ze'iri from Dihavat: we learn it from 11:17 'He will shut the heavens … and you perish quickly': F3 — the shutting bound to the perishing (the verse's own cascade) — heavens_shut_for_turning's ground.
- Taanit 8a:18 [LINK — Deut 11:17] — DERIVATION. Reish Lakish: 'He will shut up the heavens' (11:17) — the heavens closed from dew and rain like a woman in labor who cannot give birth; bar Kappara: 'closing' said of rains and of a woman: F3 — THE SHUTTING VERB THE WOMBS' (the Sifrei 306:6's reading at its Bavli seat — the design's note confirmed; 8b:1-3 the three analogies).
- Taanit 8b:1 [LINK — Deut 11:17 — CREDITED: talmud_triage_gen_2026-09-01.md] — DERIVATION. CREDITED (talmud_triage_gen_2026-09-01.md — read whole here) — Reish Lakish: 'closing' of the wombs (Genesis 20:18) and 'closing' of the heavens (11:17): F3 — the shutting verb the wombs' (8a:18 the frame).
- Taanit 9b:11 [LINK — Deut 11:11] — DISPUTE. R. Yehoshua: the whole world drinks from the UPPER waters — 'drinks water by the rain of heaven' (11:11); the mist (Genesis 2:6) the clouds rising to receive it (Job 36:27): F2 — THE SOURCE OF RAIN a PARAMETER (R. - Berakhot 14a:1 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the breaks between the paragraphs: greet for respect, respond to anyone: F3 — the paragraphs' breaks (chapter 6's recite_how by CALL).
- Berakhot 14a:2 [TOPIC] — LAW. the baraita: R. Meir — at the breaks greet for respect, in the middle for fear; R. Yehuda — in the middle greet for fear and respond for respect, at the breaks greet for respect and respond to anyone: F3 — the interruption law of the recitation (hear_o_israel by CALL); the second paragraph a paragraph with breaks — a DATA row.
- Berakhot 14a:3 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Achai's dilemma: interrupt hallel (the praise psalms) and the Megillah (the scroll of Esther)? — an a-fortiori (I1) from the Shema, or the miracle's publicity: outside the chapter's matter.
- Berakhot 14a:4 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. R. Chiyya: one interrupts; Rabba — days of the whole hallel between paragraphs, days of the partial even inside: the hallel's law.
- Berakhot 14a:5 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Rav bar Shaba before Ravina — no interruption.
- Berakhot 14a:6 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Rav bar Shaba not important to Ravina.
- Berakhot 14a:7 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Ashyan's dilemma: may one who fasts taste the dish?
- Berakhot 14a:8 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. R. Ami: he tastes; tasting needs no blessing.
- Berakhot 14a:9 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. how much may he taste?
- Berakhot 14a:10 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. up to a quarter of a log (a measure).
- Berakhot 14a:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav: greeting a man before praying builds an altar (Isaiah 2:22 — 'how' read 'an altar', the do-not-read reading named, no code): prayer first.
- Berakhot 14a:12 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Shmuel: 'how' literal — how did you honor him before God?
- Berakhot 14a:13 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Sheshet's objection from our mishna: the breaks allow greeting before the Amida (the standing prayer).
- Berakhot 14a:14 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Abba: the bar is setting out early to his door; a chance meeting permitted.
- Berakhot 14a:15 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Yona in R. Zeira's name: one's own affairs before prayer — prohibited (not an altar).
- Berakhot 14a:16 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Idi bar Avin: righteousness before Him, then the path (Psalm 85:14).
- Berakhot 14a:17 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. whoever prays first, the Holy One tends his affairs.
- Berakhot 14a:18 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. seven nights without a dream — evil ('satisfied' read 'seven', the do-not-read reading named): the dream's lore.
- Berakhot 14a:19 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. one who fills himself with Torah and sleeps hears no evil tidings.
- Berakhot 14a:20 [TOPIC] — LAW. the breaks: between the last paragraph and the blessing after it — the first tanna permits, R. Yehuda forbids: F3 — the second paragraph's boundary with the third; the interruption law (hear_o_israel by CALL).
- Berakhot 14a:21 [TOPIC] — LAW. R. Abbahu in R. Yochanan's name: the halakha as R. Yehuda — no interruption between 'your God' and 'true and firm'; the reason follows: F3 — the reading's seam.
- Berakhot 14b:1 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. 'and the LORD God is true' (Jeremiah 10:10): the seam's proof-text.
- Berakhot 14b:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. does one repeat 'true'?
- Berakhot 14b:3 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Yochanan: repeats; Rabba: not — 'every truth has caught this one'.
- Berakhot 14b:4 [TOPIC] — LAW. Rav Shmuel bar Yehuda: in the Land at evening they do not say the whole fringes paragraph (no fringes at night) but its head and its end: F3 — the THIRD paragraph's night form; the paragraphs' set a DATA row (the second said whole both times).
- Berakhot 14b:5 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Abaye: Rav Kahana in Rav's name — begun, one completes it; 'speak to the children of Israel' not a beginning, 'and say to them' is.
- Berakhot 14b:6 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Pappa: in the Land not a beginning until 'make for them fringes'.
- Berakhot 14b:7 [TOPIC] — LAW. Abaye: therefore we begin the fringes paragraph as they do in the Land, and complete it: F3 — the third paragraph at night (a DATA row).
- Berakhot 14b:8 [TOPIC] — LAW. Chiyya bar Rav: the evening's 'I am the LORD your God' needs 'true and faithful'; else not.
- Berakhot 14b:9 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. must the exodus be mentioned at night?
- Berakhot 14b:10 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the shorter formula — 'we give thanks … who took us out from Egypt' — covers it.
- Berakhot 14b:11 [TOPIC] — LAW. THE CROWN OF F3: THE MISHNAH'S REASON — R. Yehoshua ben Korcha: why does the Shema precede 'and it shall be, if you hearken'? to accept the yoke of the kingdom of Heaven first, then THE YOKE OF THE COMMANDMENTS: F3 — yoke_of_the_commandments_accepted NAMED BY THE ANSWER SHEET (Mishnah Berakhot 2:2 credited; the Gemara's citation the seat): the second paragraph's status.
- Berakhot 14b:12 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Shimon ben Yochai's other reason: the Shema holds the directive to LEARN, 'if you hearken' to TEACH, the fringes to PERFORM — the order by the directives: F3 — the paragraph's content typed (to teach — 11:19); the exam's row.
- Berakhot 14b:14 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. corrected: the Shema — learn, teach, perform; 'if you hearken' — teach and perform; the fringes — perform: F3 — the second paragraph's TWO DIRECTIVES (teach 11:19; perform 11:18, 11:20) — the design's rows by CALL confirmed (14b:13 the link row, credited).
- Berakhot 14b:15 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the two reasons compatible — one reason and another.
- Berakhot 14b:16 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav washed, recited the Shema, donned tefillin, prayed; the grave-digger's exemption baraita.
- Berakhot 14b:17 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the baraita contradictory.
- Berakhot 14b:18 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. two diggers / one.
- Berakhot 14b:19 [TOPIC] — LAW. Rav as R. Yehoshua ben Korcha: the yoke of the kingdom first, then the yoke of the commandments — so the Shema before tefillin: F3 — the yoke's ORDER applied to performance (the status's use).
- Berakhot 14b:20 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the challenge: precedence of recitation over recitation, not over performance.
- Berakhot 14b:21 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Chiyya bar Ashi: Rav washed, blessed, taught, donned, recited.
- Berakhot 14b:22 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the blessing on study even for mishna.
- Berakhot 14b:23 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the messenger late with the tefillin.
- Berakhot 14b:24 [TOPIC] — LAW. Ulla: the Shema without tefillin — false testimony against himself; R. Yochanan: a burnt offering without its meal offering: F3 — the paragraph's tefillin clause (11:18) bound to its recitation; the exam's row.
- Berakhot 14b:25 [TOPIC] — LAW. R. Yochanan: one who seeks the complete yoke of the kingdom of Heaven (continues 15a:1, credited — relieves himself, washes, dons tefillin, recites, prays): F3 — the yoke's full form.

## Ketubot
- Ketubot 110b:1 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. the wife not moved town to city (the marriage law).
- Ketubot 110b:2 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. noxious to pleasant residence; Rabban Shimon ben Gamliel: a pleasant residence tests.
- Ketubot 110b:3 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. why not town to city?
- Ketubot 110b:4 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. R. Yosei bar Chanina: dwelling in cities is hard (Nehemiah 11:2).
- Ketubot 110b:5 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Shmuel: a change of habits or residence the start of intestinal disease; Ben Sira.
- Ketubot 110b:6 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Ben Sira on the poor's nights.
- Ketubot 110b:7 [TOPIC] — LAW. THE MISHNAH (Ketubot 13:11): ALL MAY FORCE ASCENT TO THE LAND, none may force leaving; to Jerusalem likewise — men and women alike: F4 the_borders_and_the_dread — THE DWELLING WEIGHED (80:4-5's 'possess it and dwell in it', 11:31): the Land's residence a coercible duty — the exam's row for 11:31's clause.
- Ketubot 110b:8 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. the marriage contract's currency — the Land / Cappadocia.
- Ketubot 110b:9 [TOPIC] — LAW. 'all may force ascent' includes slaves (Hebrew slaves).
- Ketubot 110b:10 [TOPIC] — LAW. includes from a pleasant residence abroad to a noxious one in the Land.
- Ketubot 110b:11 [TOPIC] — LAW. 'none may remove' includes a Canaanite slave who fled to the Land — 'sell him here' (the settling's commandment): F4 — the dwelling's rule on the slave.
- Ketubot 110b:12 [TOPIC] — LAW. to Jerusalem — from pleasant to noxious.
- Ketubot 110b:13 [TOPIC] — LAW. from Jerusalem — not even noxious to pleasant.
- Ketubot 110b:14 [TOPIC] — LAW. the baraita: he wishes to ascend, she not — forced, or divorced without her contract; she wishes, he not — forced, or he divorces with the contract: F4 — the dwelling's rule between spouses (the exam's persons).
- Ketubot 110b:15 [TOPIC] — LAW. she wishes to leave, he not — forced to stay; he wishes to leave, she not — forced to stay or divorces with the contract.
- Ketubot 110b:16 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. the currency's contradiction.
- Ketubot 110b:17 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. the place of the lien / of the collection.
- Ketubot 110b:18 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Rabba: a leniency of the marriage contract (rabbinic).
- Ketubot 110b:19 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. Rabban Shimon ben Gamliel: by Torah law — the place of the obligation.
- Ketubot 110b:20 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. promissory notes by the place written; coins unspecified.
- Ketubot 110b:21 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. 'not so for a marriage contract'.
- Ketubot 110b:22 [TOPIC] — OUTSIDE. coins, not silver strips; no small coins.
- Ketubot 110b:23 [TOPIC] — LAW. THE CROWN OF (80:4-5): a person should always reside in the Land, even in a gentile city, and not outside, even in a Jewish city — whoever resides in the Land is as one who has a God, outside as one who has none: 'to give you the land of Canaan, TO BE YOUR GOD' (Leviticus 25:38): F4 — 11:31's 'dwell in it' carried to its rule: the dwelling weighed against all the commandments (the Sifrei 80:4-5 read at chapter 11's reading) — the exam's crown row.
- Ketubot 110b:24 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. no God? — rather, as one who worships idols: David's 'go, serve other gods' (1 Samuel 26:19): F4 — 11:16's 'serve other gods' the shelf's own likeness for dwelling abroad — a DATA row (other_gods_barred by CALL).
- Ketubot 110b:25 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Zeira avoided Rav Yehuda — ascending from Babylonia transgresses a positive command (continues 111a:1): the dwelling's counter-arm.
- Ketubot 111a:1 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. 'to Babylonia they shall be taken and there remain' (Jeremiah 27:22) — Rav Yehuda; R. Zeira: the vessels: F4 — THE DWELLING'S DISPUTE (the Land against the exile's decree) — the parameter's two arms.
- Ketubot 111a:2 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. Rav Yehuda: the oath 'not to awaken love' (Song of Songs 2:7).
- Ketubot 111a:3 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Zeira: not as a wall — individuals may; Rav Yehuda: the second oath (3:5).
- Ketubot 111a:4 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. R. Yosei son of R. Chanina: THE THREE OATHS — not as a wall, not to rebel against the nations, the nations not to subjugate excessively: a DATA row (the run's rule on the return).
- Ketubot 111a:5 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. 'that you not awaken' — the amplification.
- Ketubot 111a:6 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. R. Levi: six oaths — the end not revealed, not distanced, the secret not told.
- Ketubot 111a:7 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Elazar: the gazelles and hinds — the flesh abandoned if the oath is broken.
- Ketubot 111a:8 [TOPIC] — DERIVATION. R. Elazar: whoever resides in the Land dwells without sin (Isaiah 33:24); Rava: of the sick.
- Ketubot 111a:9 [TOPIC — CREDITED: EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md, ordinances_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md] — DERIVATION. CREDITED (EXOD_TALMUD_TRIAGE_LEDGER.md, ordinances_topic_docket_2026-09-06.md — read whole here) — Rav Anan: buried in the Land as under the altar — 'an altar of EARTH' (Exodus 20:21) / 'atones for the LAND of His people' (32:43), I2: F4 — the Land's burial (the dwelling's sugya).
- Ketubot 111a:10 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Ulla died outside — 'you shall die in an unclean land' (Amos 7:17); absorbed alive is not absorbed dead.
- Ketubot 111a:11 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the levirate widow in Bei Choza'a — may he descend?
- Ketubot 111a:12 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. R. Chanina: his brother married a Samaritan and died — do not follow.
- Ketubot 111a:13 [TOPIC] — LAW. Rav Yehuda in Shmuel's name: as leaving the Land for Babylonia is forbidden, so leaving Babylonia for other lands; Pumbedita to Bei Kuvei excommunicated: F4 — the dwelling's rule extended (Babylonia's own case) — a DATA row.
- Ketubot 111a:14 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the worthy of Babylonia absorbed by the Land — burial; the lineages.
- Ketubot 111a:15 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. Rav Yehuda: residing in Babylon as in the Land (Zechariah 2:11); Hutzal's horn of salvation.
- Ketubot 111a:16 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Elazar: THE DEAD OUTSIDE THE LAND DO NOT RISE (Ezekiel 26:20 'glory' read 'My desire'): F4 — the resurrection bound to the Land (11:21's 'to give to them' the resurrection's seat — 47:2, Sanhedrin 90b:12 credited): the dispute's first arm.
- Ketubot 111a:17 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Abba bar Memel: 'your dead shall live; my dead bodies shall arise' (Isaiah 26:19) — the outside's dead too; Ezekiel's 'glory' Nebuchadnezzar.
- Ketubot 111a:18 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Elazar from Isaiah 42:5 'spirit to they who walk there'; 'my dead bodies' the stillborn.

- Tosefta Sotah 8:1 [TOPIC] — LAW. the blessings and curses at the crossing (27:2 'on the day you cross') — the Ark before the banners, the priests carrying it (Joshua 3:11, 13); R. Yosei's three carryings: F5 — THE CEREMONY'S DAY THE CROSSING'S (the debit's closer dated by the Tosefta — Joshua 4:19's tenth of Nisan) — a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sotah 8:2 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. the Jordan's waters piled twelve mil (a mile — R. Yehuda) / three hundred (R. Elazar bar Shimon: water lighter than man); the kings heard; Rahab's speech (Joshua 2:10-11): Rahab the run's receipt of 11:4's sea — a DATA row.
- Tosefta Sotah 8:3 [TOPIC] — LAW. in the Jordan Joshua's condition — 'for this you cross: to dispossess (Numbers 33:52-56); if not, the waters wash over you'; the twelve stones from under the priests' feet (Joshua 4:3-9), forty seahs (a measure) each — the spies' grapes measured from them; the waters returned: F4 — THE DISPOSSESSION AS THE CONDITION OF THE CROSSING (11:23's promise a term at the Jordan) — borders and seven_nations by CALL; the run's receipt.
- Tosefta Sotah 8:4 [TOPIC] — CONTEXT. three sets of stones — Moses' at the Jordan's bank on the plains of Moab (27:2), the priests' feet, the carried ones: a DATA row (27:2-4's stones at chapter 27's compile).
- Tosefta Sotah 8:5 [TOPIC] — DISPUTE. R. Yehuda: written on the altar's stones — the nations' scribes copied the seventy languages, then were sealed to the pit; R. Shimon: on the plaster, written below, for 'lest they teach you' (20:17-18) — HAD THEY REPENTED, ACCEPTED: F5 — the writing's PLACE a parameter (chapter 7's Sotah 35b:10 credited the Bavli seat).
- Tosefta Sotah 8:7 [TOPIC] — LAW. the ceremony's form whole: six and six, the priests, the Levites and the Ark below, Gerizim's more numerous (Levi partly below); R. Eliezer ben Yaakov's resolution; Rabbi's (the fit to minister below); the blessing first and the Amen; 'cursed be he who does not uphold' a general curse; four by four, eight and eight, sixteen by sixteen, three covenants each — FORTY-EIGHT COVENANTS, and so on the plains of Moab (R. Shimon: the Tent of Meeting in, Gerizim and Ebal out); 603,550 covenants per matter, the exodus' number (the text's 503,550 the editor's correction): F5 — THE CEREMONY'S ARITHMETIC — the covenants counted from the form — a DATA row (Sotah 37b:1-6 credited the Bavli's).

## The finds (this docket's crowns)
- THE YOKE OF THE COMMANDMENTS NAMED BY THE ANSWER SHEET (Mishnah Berakhot 2:2; Berakhot 14b:11, 14b:12-14, 14b:19, 14b:24; 13a-b credited): the second paragraph after the first — the yoke of the kingdom of Heaven, then THE YOKE OF THE COMMANDMENTS (R. Yehoshua ben Korcha); R. Shimon ben Yochai's typing — the first paragraph learn, teach and perform, the second TEACH AND PERFORM (11:18-20), the third perform only; Rav's practice, the Shema before the tefillin, the yoke's order applied; the Shema without tefillin false testimony against oneself: yoke_of_the_commandments_accepted's NAME AND VALUE CONFIRMED; the paragraph's two directives the design's rows by CALL.
- THE PRAYER FROM THE PARAGRAPH'S OWN CLAUSE, AND THE RAIN ATTACHED TO IT (Ta'anit 2a:11; Berakhot 33a:11, 33a:13, 33a:29; Ta'anit 2a:1-5, 6a:5, 6a:7, 10a:11-16): "to serve Him with all your heart" (11:13) is PRAYER, and "I will give the rain of your land" (11:14) follows it — the request for rain in the service of the heart; the mention in the resurrection blessing (rain a revival), the request in the years' blessing (sustenance), both INDISPENSABLE (omitted, one returns to the beginning); THE DATES, EVERY ONE FROM A ROW: the mention from the last day of the feast (R. Yehoshua; Mishnah Ta'anit 1:1-2 through 2a:1-3) to Passover's first day; the request from the third of Marcheshvan, or the seventh (Rabban Gamliel — the halakha; the last pilgrim to the Euphrates), in the diaspora sixty days into the season (Chananya — the halakha); the early rain in Marcheshvan and the late in Nisan ("in its due time"); the fasts from the seventeenth of Marcheshvan and the New Moon of Kislev: rain_dates' VALUES.
- THE DECREE FIXED AT THE HEAD OF THE YEAR, THE DISTRIBUTION FREE (Rosh Hashanah 17b:11-13, 8a:16, 16b:12, 16b:3; Ta'anit 8b:5-6): "the eyes of the LORD your God are always on it, from the beginning of the year to its end" (11:12) — the year judged at its head (the three books; the New Year for years is for judgment); the rain decreed cannot be added to or reduced, but falls IN SEASON on the land that needs it for good, or OUT OF SEASON on forests and deserts for bad: "IN ITS SEASON" (11:14) THE FREE VARIABLE AFTER THE DECREE — rain_in_its_season's value made precise; the mountains or the fields (8b:5); the year poor at its head rich at its end (the defective spelling, 16b:3).
- THE SHUTTING'S VALUE, THRESHOLD AND CAUSES (Ta'anit 3b:6; 6b:8; 8a:18, 8b:1-3; 7b:5-8a:8, 8a:14, 8b:16; 7b:4): "He will shut up the heavens" (11:17) is THE CLOUDS AND THE WINDS WITHHELD, not rain alone (Rav Yosef's baraita); the threshold — rains that seal a barrel's mouth with mud are not "shut" (Rav Chisda); the shutting verb the wombs' (Reish Lakish in bar Kappara's name — closing, birth, remembering: three analogies to the woman — the Sifrei 306:6 at its Bavli seat); THE CAUSES ON THE SHELF beside 11:16's "serve other gods" — the sentence of destruction (11:17, Ze'iri), the tithes annulled, slander, the impudent, the neglect of Torah, robbery, no whisperers of prayer, false pledges of charity; the remedy prayer and the generation's pious; Solomon's dedication the run's receipt ("heaven shut up and no rain", 1 Kings 8:35-36): heavens_shut_for_turning's VALUE and the exam's cause rows — the cause a PARAMETER with many arms.
- THE LAND WATERED BY HEAVEN, FIRST (Ta'anit 10a:2-3; 9b:10-16, 10a:4-7; Bava Batra 19a:18; Shabbat 85a:3; Berakhot 8a:6; Kiddushin 26a:10): the Land created first and watered by the Holy One Himself, the world by an intermediary, the Land drinking first and the world the residue (Job 5:10 — the Sifrei 37-40's baraita at its Bavli seat); THE SOURCE OF RAIN A DISPUTE — R. Eliezer the ocean sweetened in the clouds, R. Yehoshua the upper waters, 11:11 HIS VERSE; no water channels needed in the Land; "with your foot" a handbreadth; the long days in the Land and not outside; "possess it and dwell in it" (11:31) an act of acquisition.
- THE CATTLE BEFORE THE MAN (Berakhot 40a:1; Gittin 62a:16; Ta'anit 8b:14): "grass in your field for your cattle", then "you shall eat and be satisfied" (11:15) — PROHIBITED TO EAT BEFORE FEEDING ONE'S ANIMALS: a rule from the verse's clause order (Rav Yehuda in Rav's name at two seats); the granary's blessing before the measuring.
- THE PARAGRAPH'S DUTIES GRADED BY THE CLASS RULE (Mishnah Kiddushin 1:7; Kiddushin 34a:2, 34a:8; Yoma 11b:8; Kiddushin 36a:9; Bava Batra 21a:2, 110b:7; Shabbat 32b:4; Rosh Hashanah 17a:10): the mezuzah not time-bound — women obligated, the reward "that your days be multiplied" deciding against the juxtaposition to study; the tefillin time-bound — women exempt, and Isi's baldness bar with them ("between your eyes" / "between your eyes", I2); "you shall teach them" read "you yourselves" — the father, and Yehoshua ben Gamla's schools; "your sons" inclusive at the reward; children die for the mezuzah's neglect or for the study's — the adjacency's reach (E28 from-the-preceding); the skull that never donned tefillin the body's rebellion.
- THE CEREMONY — ITS PLACE THREE WAYS, ITS FORM, ITS TONGUE, ITS ARITHMETIC (Sotah 32a:10-13, 33b:4-12, 36b:4-5, 37a:7-11; Tosefta Sotah 8:1-7; Sotah 32a:6-7, 33a:13-14, 33b:1-2): 11:30's clauses read by R. Yehuda as SHECHEM (the terebinths of Moreh — Genesis 12:6, I2; the Samaritans' forged "Shechem" conceded and refuted, 33b:6), by R. Elazar as TWO MOUNDS BY THE JORDAN (27:4 the same day; the Arabah; Gilgal in sight; Shechem the Hivites', not "the Canaanite's"), by R. Eliezer ben Yaakov as A ROUTE, NOT A PLACE (the way, the settled, the plain — as the cloud showed the way the first time): gerizim_ebal_place's THREE ARMS; the form — six and six, the priests, the Levites and the Ark below, the Levites' placement disputed (the elders below / the fit / all below, "on" the mount meaning beside), the blessing first and the Amen, the general and the particular, to learn, to teach, to keep, to perform — FORTY-EIGHT COVENANTS (and 603,550 per matter); the holy tongue — "speak and say" the source for others' Hebrew, the Levites' own from "voice" / "voice" (Exodus 19:19); the day the crossing's (Tosefta 8:1); the stones and the seventy languages (on the stones / on the plaster — Tosefta 8:5); the dispossession the crossing's condition (Tosefta 8:3): gerizim_ebal_ceremony_owed's value and its closer described.
- THE RECEIPT'S POINTER TAUGHT A SECOND TIME (Tosefta Sotah 8:6; Pesachim 8b:7-9): "no man could stand before them … 'I will send My terror before you' (Exodus 23:27)" — the Tosefta joins 11:25's sentence to Exodus 23:27 as the Sifrei 52:4 did: the design's RUN_CITATION pointer with two teachers; the pilgrimage's guard (Exodus 34:24 — the cow, the rooster; going and returning by 16:7; one who has land ascends) at its Bavli seat, pilgrim_land_guarded's teacher.
- THE DWELLING WEIGHED, AND ITS COUNTER-ARM (Mishnah Ketubot 13:11; Ketubot 110b:7-15, 110b:23-24, 111a:1-8, 111a:13-21): all may force ascent, none removal; residing outside the Land as idolatry — David's "go, serve other gods" (11:16's phrase); against it Rav Yehuda's exile decree (Jeremiah 27:22) and the three (six) oaths — not as a wall; the dead outside not rising (R. Elazar; the tunnels) against Isaiah 26:19 — the resurrection from 11:21's "to them" (Sanhedrin 90b credited) at its Land-bound edge: 11:31's "dwell in it" the exam's row (the Sifrei 80:4-5).
- STUDY GREATER, FOR IT LEADS TO DEED (Kiddushin 40b:5, 40b:8-12): the loft at Lod — the Sifrei 41:12's ruling at its Bavli seat; the Torah's precedence counted on the clock (the dough-portion, the tithes, the sabbatical, the jubilee — the land laws after the conquest and the division); the judgment and the reward begin with Torah.
- THE SHEMA'S LANGUAGE AND ORDER (Sotah 32a:5, 32b:19-33a:4, 33a:5-7): the paragraphs in any language (the Rabbis) or as written (Rabbi); not out of order ("and these words will be"); audible ("hear"); the Amida in any language but not Aramaic for the individual: the exam's rows for the paragraph's recitation.
- WHAT THE DESIGN DID NOT PREDICT (carried to RUN B as DATA rows or values): the shutting's VALUE (the clouds and the winds) and THRESHOLD (the barrel's mud); "in its season" THE FREE VARIABLE AFTER THE DECREE; the cattle-before-the-man rule from the clause order; the receipt's pointer taught a second time by the Tosefta; the ceremony's place a THREE-ARM parameter (Shechem / the Jordan's mounds / a route); the ceremony's day the crossing's, its tongue from "voice" / "voice", its forty-eight covenants; the dispossession as the crossing's condition; the Samaritan "Shechem" conceded by R. Elazar son of R. Yosei; the rain's source a dispute with 11:11 R.
